## 2021

### Monday, November 8, 2021

[Meeting page](../../meetings/2021/2021-11-08.md) · [Full transcript](../../transcripts/2021/2021-11-08.md) · [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=72wEMmTdpKc) · South Lyon City Council Nov 8 2021

[0:22:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=72wEMmTdpKc&t=1347s) Chief, I wanted to thank you personally, as a father of two, my daughter's got a real Kick out of the police coming through the neighborhood, even lifting them up in the Police cruiser to at least get the candy out of the officer's hand. Those are priceless interactions that honestly, money can't buy. So I want to echo what Glenn already touched on earlier, and thank you so much for letting Letting your officers be a part of my neighborhood, Personally, and neighborhoods in the area. Thank you.

[1:02:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=72wEMmTdpKc&t=3766s) Second, Steve's motion and also kind of build upon it by saying this, the service there Was a little bit of a hiccup there but obviously you've overcome it and I definitely haven't Heard many complaints in quite a few number of months from my neighbors. I will say this from experience in in my past neighborhood when the service was changed Changed from one waste hauler to another. The headache of exchanging carts, Making sure that you've got the old carts ready for pickup. And that whole transition was a big hardship To the entire neighborhood. And that was only a couple of hundred houses. So I can only imagine what would be as far as a headache To our entire city's residents. So I figured I'd at least toss that anecdote out there To the rest of council. And in addition, another note that I made, I loved Steve's idea about increasing the number Of available recycling carts uh with the continuation of uh an extension of the contract

[1:14:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=72wEMmTdpKc&t=4481s) I want to thank everyone for welcoming me so much to the to the Council, and I wanted to thank all the volunteers and folks that helped to get all those votes counted and to handle the election seamlessly. I would have loved to have seen a lot more turnout, but it is an off -off year, so that's Definitely probably par for the course. Wanted to thank again the police chief for letting his crew come out into the neighborhoods And pass out candy. My daughters are still thinking about it, at least my three -and -a -half -year -old. My one -month -old isn't really talking about it too much, but you can't keep kids from Picking up candy in even rain or shine. So luckily the weather eventually did work out for us so again I'm humbled to be in the in this role In this position and I'm looking forward to serving the city and the residents of south Lyons so thank you all again and uh look forward to uh the years ahead thank you Councilmember

[1:08:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rHAvXTgp1gI&t=4112s) Well I I just had a couple things to comment on um the other day I was picking up my daughters and I was originally going to take them to McCaddy Park unfortunately it was Well over 95 degrees with probably 110 percent humidity kidding but instead of That we decided to go to sponge sugar and I probably spent too much money and I definitely wanted to give a shout out to you know that business as well as my Kids being well my four -year -old my one -year -old is a little bit too young But my four -year -old was definitely elated with the selection there so Wanted to give that good good thumbs up kid approved so the other thing I wanted To mention is a little bit more somber and depressing I don't know if council is aware but the Huron River is a 130 mile long river in southeast Michigan rising out of the Huron swamp in Springfield Township in northern Oakland County and flowing into Lake Erie as it forms the boundary between present -day Wayne and Monroe counties now 13 parks game areas and recreation areas are associated with the river which passes through the cities of Dexter Ann Arbor Ypsilanti Bel Air Flat Rock and Rockwood that were developed along its banks now the Huron River is a typical southeast Michigan stream mud banks slow stream Flowing and a low gradient to find the river it runs through the following counties in order from the headwaters to the mouth Oakland Livingston Washtenaw Wayne and Monroe now there are 24 major tributaries totaling about 370 miles in addition to the mainstream now the Huron River watershed which Southline is a Part of drains 908 square miles now it is the only state designated County Scenic natural river in southeast Michigan this includes 27 .5 miles of the The mainstream, plus an additional 10 .5 miles of Three tributaries. Now, the Huron River flows through numerous parks. It is a prime canoeing river with a generally slow Current with only a few minor rapids or Obstructions, and it is also heavily fished by Sportsmen for several different species of fish. Now, the reason I bring this up is we are Are continuing to find out more information regarding the dumping of hexavalent chromium into the Huron River and the Huron River watershed by Wixom -based Tribar Manufacturing. Now we know they did it because they told us they did it. Now I'm appalled at this disregard for our environment, but why should we be surprised? This is the same company that was found to be dumping dangerous levels of PFAS into the same watershed in 2018. So much that residents were advised to avoid foam and advised to not eat the fish. Many of the notices are still displayed around the area. Now here we are again, and only worse. We're being told to avoid contact, avoid contact with the water. better. The movie Aaron Brock depicts seems to come to mind with this. Now I'm disgusted that we are finding ourselves here yet again. Now when I lived in Livingston County, I begged the county commission for action. They did practically nothing. So I truly hope that This time we, and I say we, as a collective body, the state, the county government, city Municipalities, such as ourselves, hold those responsible and accountable. Thank you.
